South Central Cannabis & Hemp Business Directory — Audited and Corrected

Region: Kentucky, Tennessee, Alabama, Mississippi, Louisiana and Arkansas. Last audited: 28 October 2026. Original report: South Central Cannabis and Hemp Business Directory, Complete (Categories 1–12), all entries accessed 25 October 2026 in the original, 147 unique main entries. This audit: every entry re-checked against its own website first, then state registries and credential lookups where a result could be obtained (see Method). Result: 147 entries audited — Confirmed: 31, Corrected: 43, Unverified: 56, Removed: 15, Already listed: 2. Rewritten report below keeps 130 entries (147 minus 15 Removed minus 2 Already listed merged into entry 9). Unverified entries are KEPT in the rewritten report, clearly flagged — they are not silently dropped, and they must not be used for outreach without the re-check named in the audit table.

Read this before using any entry for outreach. This audit is honest about its limits, and those limits are part of the verdicts. (1) Secretary of State registries: KY, TN, AL, MS, LA (geauxBIZ) and AR portals are interactive search tools. Text searches in this audit returned no direct SOS entity record for most entries, so this audit does not claim SOS-confirmed status for any entry unless a specific registry result is named in its row. Existence below rests on live own sites, regulator lists, or reputable reporting as stated per entry. A live-browser SOS search by exact legal name is still outstanding for the whole directory and must not be assumed from website existence alone. (2) Rate limits: on 28 October 2026 a browser-service 429 rate limit blocked many re-opens. Those calls were not retried and are not reported as site failures. Entries affected are marked Unverified, or Corrected/Confirmed only where search-indexed own-site text or the original 2026-10-25 direct open supports it, and the audit row says which. (3) Licence numbers: no licence, bar, CPA, insurance, contractor, security, or hemp licence/permit number is asserted unless a regulator or the business itself published it. Where none was published, the row says Not published / Unverified. Batch/lot numbers on Alabama ABC label lists, COA numbers, and directory licence claims are not licence numbers and are labelled as such. (4) Search snippets are not own-site opens. Where a fact rests only on search-indexed own-site text or on the original 2026-10-25 open, the row says so. (5) Personal contacts: no personal phone or email is reproduced. Published work contacts for named owners and key people are retained only where the business itself published them. Where the original gave a contact that could not be re-confirmed, this audit keeps it flagged rather than inventing a replacement.

How to read the verdicts

  • Confirmed — existence, cannabis/hemp connection, and the contacts carried forward were confirmed on the business's own site (opened 2026-10-28, or on the original 2026-10-25 open where the row says so) or another reliable public source named in the row, with no material correction. Credential numbers remain Unverified unless a specific lookup is named.
  • Corrected — the business stays, but a name, URL, tag, address, phone, scale, category, or other material detail was corrected, or a conflict was resolved/flagged as described. The correction is in the audit row and applied in the rewritten report.
  • Unverified — the business is plausibly real and is kept, flagged, but a key fact (existence, cannabis/hemp connection, contact, licence, or local office) could not be confirmed on its own site or a registry in this audit. Do not use it for outreach without the re-check named in the row.
  • Removed — removed from the directory: cannot confirm it exists or is currently operating, it has closed/been dissolved/merged/acquired (acquirer named where known), or it has no cannabis/hemp evidence on its own site or a reliable source. For general garden stores and general cash firms removed on cannabis-evidence grounds only, the row says so explicitly and gives the reinstatement condition; removal is not a claim that the underlying general business is closed.
  • Already listed — the same business appears under another entry in this directory and is merged there, not counted twice. Its distinct facts are preserved in the master entry.

Method

For every entry, in order: (1) the business's own official website was opened first (URL from the original entry; if none, the official site was searched for). If the site could not be opened, that is stated and the entry is marked Verify/Unverified as appropriate — search-result snippets, cached summaries, and directory listings alone were not accepted as a substitute for an own-site open, and where they were used they are labelled as search-index or prior-open evidence. (2) The business entity registry of the state of registration was checked where a search result could be obtained (Kentucky, Tennessee, Alabama, Mississippi, Louisiana geauxBIZ, Arkansas Secretaries of State); because those portals are interactive, most searches returned no direct record, and that gap is recorded rather than papered over. (3) Cannabis/hemp connection was confirmed from the business's own website or another reliable public source; it was not assumed from business type alone. For armoured transport, cash management, waste, and banking firms, specific acceptance of cannabis or hemp clients was required. (4) The cannabis/hemp/both tag was checked against the business's own website and corrected where wrong. (5) Website URL, phone, email, street address, and social links were checked against what the business publishes itself (website footer for social links) and corrected where they differ; only contacts the business publishes itself are used. (6) Every bar, CPA, contractor, security, insurance producer, hemp licence, or hemp-derived product permit number was checked against the official lookup (state licensing boards, state department of agriculture, Tennessee TABC, Kentucky ABC, Louisiana ATC/LDH, USDA hemp records) where a lookup result could be obtained; each is marked Confirmed, Corrected, or Unverified and the lookup is named. In practice, almost every credential in this directory is Unverified because no number was published or no registry result was returned — that is stated, not hidden. (7) Duplicates were merged, including under different names, DBAs, parent companies, and across states; a business serving several states is listed once, with every state it serves. (8) Licensed operators were checked for removal (see Licensed operators moved out). (9) Category 12 hemp licensees were kept only if they make or brand finished products sold to the public or other businesses, with licence/permit number recorded where published. (10) Every source link was checked and broken or wrong links replaced or struck (notably the Tree of Life Seeds domain, now gambling spam and struck from this report, and borohemp.com, suspended). Same-name traps were checked throughout (Lexington KY vs VA; Jackson MS vs TN; Columbus MS vs OH/GA; Florence AL vs KY/SC; Covington KY vs LA; Paris KY vs TN; Alexandria and Monroe LA vs other states; Fayetteville AR vs NC; Greenville MS vs NC/SC; Texarkana AR/TX; Bristol TN/VA). For cross-border metros (Memphis, Northern Kentucky/Cincinnati, Chattanooga, Bristol/Kingsport), an office in the state listed was required; where only service (not an office) was evidenced, the row says so.

Counts

Original: Category 1 — 39; Category 2 — 8; Category 3 — 13; Category 4 — 4; Category 5 — 15; Category 6 — 5; Category 7 — 4; Category 8 — 3; Category 9 — 2; Category 10 — 6; Category 11 — 17; Category 12 — 31. Run 1 (Cats 1–6) = 84; Run 2 (Cats 7–12) = 63; combined unique total = 147. Audit verdicts: Confirmed 31, Corrected 43, Unverified 56, Removed 15, Already listed 2. Rewritten report: 130 entries. Removed (15): #39 Barber Munson (historical only), #60 Malloy Insurance (no current cannabis evidence; agency still trading), #86 GardaWorld Cash (no KY/TN cannabis acceptance evidenced), #101 Success Hydroponics, #102 New Earth Organics & Hydroponics, #103 Louisville Hydroponics Garden Supply, #107 Sun City Hydroponics, #109 Hydro-Ponics of Birmingham, #110 Hydroponics360, #112 Laughing Buddha Nursery, #115 Hog City Hydro, #116 The Nurserie (all grow stores removed on cannabis-evidence grounds only — see rows), #136 Boro Hemp Co (site suspended), #137 Kengro Corporation (no current hemp finished product; kenaf only), #146 Tree of Life Seeds (domain lost to gambling spam; no current product evidence). Already listed merged (2): #10 Litson PLLC and #11 Fuson Law PC, both merged into #9 Hemp Law Group.

Overlap check — 'Already listed' in other lists

The only list attached to this audit was the South Central directory itself. A cross-check was run against the Southeast Cannabis and Hemp Business Directory (audited version) and the National Cannabis and Hemp Business Directory (audited version) held in the workspace, because the commissioning note flagged Tennessee firms that might already appear in the Southeast directory and Northern Kentucky firms that serve Ohio. Result: no Tennessee firm in this directory was found as a listed entry in the Southeast audited directory — the Southeast directory's scope is Virginia, West Virginia, North Carolina, South Carolina, Georgia, and Florida, so Tennessee is outside it; the name matches found there (e.g. Dark Horse CPAs SC-1, UHY VA offices) are different regional entries/offices, not the South Central entries, and are not marked Already listed. National firms that also appear in the National audited directory (Baker Donelson, Amwins, HUB International, Brink's/PAI, Cornbread Hemp, Victory Hemp Foods, HempWood, BastCore, Liberty Defense Group) appear there under different offices/scopes (national listings, or other regions); their South Central offices are distinct listings under this directory's rule (national companies only with a verified local office in the region, marked National), so they are not removed here on a name match alone. No Ohio directory was attached or found in the workspace, so Northern Kentucky / Cincinnati firms (Dinsmore, Reminger, Stites Covington office) could not be checked against an Ohio list; Dinsmore's KY office address remains Verify, and Reminger's Ft. Mitchell (KY) office is confirmed as a KY nexus. The only Already listed verdicts in this audit are internal to this directory: #10 and #11 merged into #9 (see audit table). If the parent holds a separate Southeast or Ohio list that was not attached here, run the name + office comparison against it before final publication and mark any exact-office duplicates Already listed at that point — do not mark a firm Already listed on a name alone.

PART 2 — REWRITTEN REPORT (CORRECTED, LAST AUDITED 28 OCTOBER 2026)

Every confirmed and corrected business from the original report is kept below, with every correction from Part 1 applied in place. Removed businesses, Already listed businesses, and licensed operators moved out are not in Part 2 (they are in Part 1, with reasons). Unverified businesses are kept, flagged [UNVERIFIED] in the Business column, with the flag explained in the Audit note column. Every business keeps a source link. Each business shows its Standard category (exactly one of the task list), its Local / Regional / National tag, and its cannabis / hemp / both tag. 'Not published' means the field was not found on the pages opened; it was never invented. Licence numbers are quoted only where a source actually published them, and are marked Confirmed, Corrected, or Unverified with the lookup named. Tables wrap rather than cut off.

Research notes carried forward (audited)

These notes from the original report are retained, with audit corrections applied. They are not a substitute for the per-entry rows in Part 1.

  • Scope exclusions applied (unchanged in principle): state-licensed cannabis operators and their holders were excluded — Kentucky medical cannabis cultivators, processors, producers, dispensaries and safety compliance facilities; Alabama Medical Cannabis Commission licensees; Mississippi MSDH licensees and MDOR-licensed dispensaries; Louisiana licensed cultivators (including university-affiliated licensees and their contractors), marijuana pharmacies and testing labs; Arkansas Medical Marijuana Commission licensees; and any tribal cannabis enterprise. Hemp growers/processors appear in Category 12 only if they make/brand finished products sold to the public or other businesses, with licence/permit number noted where published. Plain licensed retail shops that only resell other companies' products are not listed in Category 12. National companies are included only with a verified local office in the region, marked National. Businesses that serve only hemp clients count and are marked hemp-only / both as applicable. For Categories 7 and 8, specific cannabis-client acceptance is required: a general armoured-transport, cash, or waste firm with no cannabis/hemp evidence does not qualify — which is why GardaWorld Cash (#86) was removed and why Brink's/PAI (#85) is Corrected (national acceptance confirmed, KY/TN acceptance not).
  • Cross-run cross-references (not double-counted, audited): Liberty Defense Group, LLC (Cat 6, Arkansas) also offers armed cash movement / secure transport serving AR (and, per its cannabis page, MO, MS, LA) — see #84, listed once in Category 6, not duplicated in Category 7. Hemp Law Group (#9) is the joint marketing effort of Litson PLLC and Fuson Law PC — see #10–#11, merged into #9. Belcher, Sykes Harrington / Harrington Beverage Law (#95) is a law firm placed in Category 10 for its hemp-compliance counsel function — cross-reference Legal, do not duplicate in Category 1. KY Cannabis Law Group (#94) is a law firm placed in Category 10 for its licensing/compliance packages — cross-reference Legal, do not duplicate in Category 1.
  • Thin categories — structural reasons (audited, unchanged in substance): Category 7 has only 3 kept entries (PAI/Brink's, CannaFirst, FOCUS Bank) after GardaWorld's removal, and Category 8 has 3 kept entries (Hazmat, Inc., Sonny Daeg, Blackout 360), because the audit rule requires specific cannabis/hemp acceptance for cash/waste firms. Tennessee hemp disposal is normally on-farm by the grower under TDA rules; Kentucky medical licensees must themselves render waste unusable under 915 KAR 1:030/1:040/1:070 then use a generic waste removal company; hot-hemp destruction is KDA-supervised (302 KAR 50:056); Kentucky transport is licensee transport under 915 KAR 1:080 — no public third-party transporter/vendor list was located. Category 9 has only 2 kept entries (Herbal Solutions Staffing, Mississippi Cannabis School) — no KY- or TN-headquartered dedicated cannabis staffing agency was verified in the original or in this audit; the only TN row in the original (Nashville.com Cannabis Job Posting Network, Recruitology) was a borderline recruitment-advertising network, NOT a staffing/placement agency, and is not counted in this audit's kept total. Category 4 has only 4 kept entries — no KY, TN, LA, or AR cannabis-zoned brokerage entry was verified in the original or in this audit.
  • Same-name traps (audited): Lexington KY (not VA); Jackson MS (Pyron, Apex, Southern, MFE claim) vs Jackson TN (no TN Jackson entry in this audit's kept set — McLaughlin is Jackson, MS, not TN); Columbus MS vs OH/GA (no Columbus entry kept — check before adding one); Florence AL (Green Acres, #134) vs Florence KY/SC; Covington KY (Stites office) vs Covington LA (Willow Pharmacy, Region 9 — a licensee, not an entry); Paris KY vs Paris TN (HAT Paris, TN office NOT confirmed — do not list; King Law / Danielson Paris references are AR, not TN/KY); Alexandria and Monroe LA vs other states (no Alexandria/Monroe entry kept — Liberty Defense LA job postings in Monroe, Ruston, etc. are staffing activity, not offices); Fayetteville AR (Danielson, King Law, BiLD, Napa, Hog City — all AR, not NC) vs Fayetteville NC; Greenville MS vs NC/SC (no Greenville entry kept); Texarkana AR/TX and Bristol TN/VA (no Texarkana or Bristol entry kept — Bristol/Kingsport cross-border service is not evidenced for any kept entry). Memphis (TN/MS/AR metro), Northern Kentucky/Cincinnati (KY/OH metro), Chattanooga (TN/GA metro), and Bristol/Kingsport (TN/VA metro) firms are listed under the state of their verified office, with other states served noted — where only service (not an office) was evidenced, the row says so (e.g. Dinsmore Cincinnati HQ serving Northern KY, KY office address Verify; Amwins Birmingham office not own-site-verified; MFE Jackson office not verified as staffed; Zoned Properties AL office announced, not current street-verified).
  • Federal deadline flag (new in this audit, for Part 2 users): secondary legal summaries report PL 119-37 §781's 0.4 mg total-THC-per-container cap taking effect 11 December 2026 (synthetic/converted-cannabinoid exclusions 12 November 2026). A 5 mg Louisiana hemp seltzer (Louie Louie, Crescent 9, Driftee/Stratus) would exceed that cap. Re-check Louisiana beverage legality after those dates before presenting those brands as currently lawful. This does not change their 28 October 2026 hemp-derived status.

End of audited and corrected report. Last audited 28 October 2026. All corrections are in Part 1; Part 2 applies them in place and drops nothing else. Unverified entries remain flagged and must be re-checked at the source named in their Part 1 row before outreach.
